# Implemented proposals versus ProPresenter 7

Read-only code audit captured on October 26, 2026 at local main
`6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68`. Official sources were checked on
October 26, 2026. The audit branch was initially created from this commit. The
review request records its later rebase base separately. Code and spec
permalinks deliberately pin this snapshot, rather than silently claim that later
main changes were checked. This is a proposal for the lead's source review, not
an instruction to change the app.

The inventory includes exact `(proposal)` markers, qualified proposal markers,
`lead decision` wording, inherited proposal headings, and the settings-table
convention in spec 10. There are 494 explicit wording locations; the coverage
ledger records every one. A row can separate multiple defaults from the same
paragraph. Implemented parts of mixed paragraphs are described precisely; a
reference does not mean the entire paragraph is implemented.

"Implemented" means a mounted operator path or a runtime connected in main, not
a type, schema, test, TODO, or orphan helper alone. Audio, timer, prop, notice
and macro runtime rows do not claim their missing management tabs work. The
Preferences window is not mounted; its section covers consumed defaults and
persistence only. No app code, Bible text or lyrics is copied or changed.

Verdicts have a narrow scope:

- **matches PP**: the cited source explicitly establishes the stated behavior or
  option. This does not assert the same default, timing, algorithm or UI.
- **differs from PP**: the code and cited source establish a difference; the
  last cell proposes a change for review.
- **no PP source**: **not found** for the exact rule in the checked sources. The
  last cell is an explicitly suggested common-sense default. This is not
  evidence that PP lacks the feature, and it is never an invented PP behavior.

## User decisions excluded

The
[decision log](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/decisions.md)
takes precedence. This audit does not reopen the user's shortcut/default-key
policy, physical key matching, section name and empty-section/slot model,
section drag and midpoint drop rules, copible Bible
entry/completion/validation/candidate order and database choices, translation
delivery, long-passage splitting direction, clear toggles/fade choice,
quick-timer independence/reset behavior, shared bottom media-bin placement, UI
type sizes, output cursor/always-on-top/reveal/off behavior, unplug fallback
stacking, stage smart guides, or the saving-modal choice. Where those choices
still lag in code, they remain excluded rather than become PP findings. Numeric
defaults not decided by the user, such as zoom bounds, and explicitly recorded
lead defaults, such as the saving-modal delay, remain in scope. They are not
proposed changes to the user's chosen feature itself.

## Sources and access limits

The exact Internet Archive capture named by spec 10 S4 was retrieved and read:
7.5, April 27, 2021, 193 printed pages. The capture has five front-matter pages,
so printed p.190 is PDF page195. A raw `id_` retrieval of that same capture was
used because the normal archive view failed in the browsing tool. The guide and
extracted text remain ignored scratch files and are not committed.

Official support pages were read directly. Some live `learn.renewedvision.com`
chapter URLs now redirect to the generic knowledge base. Their indexed PP7
chapter text was readable, and the same 7.5 guide provides a stable fallback;
the redirect itself supplies no behavioral evidence. Support articles may be
newer than 7.5. An undated article does not prove when a feature was introduced
or that a current screenshot exactly matches 7.5. No PP6, third-party forum,
EasyWorship or OBS behavior is used as a PP7 result.

For **not found** rows, the citation names the closest official material
checked, not a source claiming the negative. Searches also paired the specific
rule with its feature, for example Korean initials/ranking, live-edit timing,
Identify timeout, missing-Look fallback, timer clock/date/overrun, macro batch
and recursion, save debounce/recovery, import encoding, and output restart
backoff. Internal implementation details are often undocumented.

## Differences to review first

- P03: Rank title matches before lyric matches, with title sorting within each
  group. PP library search results are alphabetical. [SEARCH].
- P10: Arrangements are individual slide-number sequences with repeats; 기본 is
  file order. PP arranges Group tokens. [ARR].
- P21: Service chips append/reorder individual slides; 기본 chips are read-only.
  PP arrangement order uses Group tokens. [ARR].
- C02: Identify shows separate 240 DIP number cards centered on physical
  monitors for three seconds, without focus/input. PP Identify overlays
  screen/output names and technical data (printed p.33). [GUIDE].
- C11: NDI is visible but disabled with a reason in this version. PP offers NDI
  outputs. [SCREEN].
- T03: All countdowns currently overrun below zero; no per-timer stop-at-zero
  choice. PP has an Overrun option. [TIMER].
- T07: Countdown-to and elapsed-from anchor to the run-start local day,
  including across midnight. PP's example selects noon tomorrow. [TIMER].
- T09: Elapsed-from is time since a wall-clock time, not a stopwatch initialized
  with a start value. PP Elapsed Time counts from a start value. [TIMER].
- T16: Audio has no device selector; development/tests mute playback, while
  packaged playback can use the system default. PP selects Main and Inspector
  output devices. [AUDIOOUT].
- E32: Media import always copies into managed media storage; no off option. PP
  Manage Media Automatically defaults off (printed p.190). [GUIDE].
- M09: PowerPoint import is visibly disabled with a reason. PP7 Windows can
  import PowerPoint slides as images. [IMPORT].
- S26: A label jump finds the next matching slide after live, wrapping to the
  first match. PP Hot Keys target a particular slide (printed p.58). [GUIDE].

## 진행 도구

Stage and Message tabs are mounted. Other rows explicitly describe connected
runtime behavior; their management tabs are not mounted.

## 환경설정

Consumed defaults/storage are covered here. A serialized setting without a
consumer, and the unmounted Preferences UI, do not count.

| ID  | Implemented behavior and spec                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Code evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | What PP7 does/source                                                 | Verdict and proposed default/change                                                                                                                                    |

Avoid backup work while any screen output is active.                                                                                                 |

## Unmounted and partial areas

- Migration-specific backup before rewriting an older schema is not connected.
  The
  [migration helper](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/src/shared/schema/migration.ts#L24)
  transforms data, while
  [FileWriter](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/src/main/store/fileWriter.ts#L22)
  queues writes. Ordinary recovery-copy support does not establish this
  migration-specific flow.
- Launch backup returns a failure outcome and logs it. Its logger does not
  publish an in-app problem;
  [registerSaveProblems](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/src/main/store/saveProblems.ts#L15)
  handles document saves, not backups. Backup warning delivery remains
  unimplemented.
- [backUpNow](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/src/main/store/backup.ts#L89)
  has an output-on guard but no operator or IPC caller. Main only
  [connects Backups.atLaunch](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/src/main/store/register.ts#L442),
  so manual backup is an unconnected helper.
- `showService` currently projects song tiles; non-song service-item editing and
  media/notice cue tiles do not meet their proposed UI. Their registered runtime
  or quick-add data behavior is listed only where implemented.
- `ShowToolsPanel` mounts Stage and Message. Showing/Audio/Timer/Notice/Prop/
  Macro management, macro collections/action palette and scheduling are not
  mounted. Registered playback/overlay/macro commands are a separate scope.
- Look/style lifecycle helpers and layout schemas do not mount a Look or stage
  editor. Stage image/memo display modes are explicit renderer TODOs. Saved text
  layouts and box styling do render, as listed above.
- File text import is mounted on this main snapshot, unlike check3. A saved
  import draft still needs explicit Save. Show-strip media import remains
  disabled, while preparation media import works. The shared-bin replacement is
  a user decision and is not judged against the old layout here.
- `previewFps` and hardware-acceleration schemas/defaults do not establish a
  working renderer preference. There is no connected preview-FPS throttle,
  layout-reset command, updater, diagnostic-copy UI or Preferences window.
- The text-import dialog uses its current bounds (including a 32-character
  symbol limit and up to 1000 units), not all spec 10's proposed tighter
  settings ranges. Working delimiter semantics are covered; the unimplemented
  preference ranges and remembered-last-import settings are not called matches.
- The proposed NDI-to-capture compatibility fallback is absent; NDI is skipped
  by the runtime source. Its visibly disabled property option is implemented.
- An item start with its macro/background/first slide is not one published batch
  in the current sender. Macro action execution itself is batched.
- The saving modal is the user's decision. Only lead-selected delay/refusal/
  timeout details are compared; no alternative to that modal is proposed.

Each explicit wording location is accounted for below. `Covered` points to the
relevant implemented portion, not every statement in its surrounding paragraph.
`User decision` skips a decided rule even if code still needs to catch up.
`Not implemented` requires a real connected path before a PP verdict. `Context`
and `Container` are proposal/research headings or rollout notes, not extra
operator behaviors. Inherited behavior and implicit settings rows are
additionally tracked after this ledger.

## Inherited proposal sections and implicit settings

New-song/deletion, label management, quick-list management and text-import child
behavior is included under the proposal-headed sections even when an individual
sentence has no marker. Spec 10 says settings rows are proposals unless their
basis is Kept; the following covers that table-level scope. A runtime-only
default does not imply its Preferences control exists.

| Settings/section range                                                                                                                               | Implemented portion                                                                       | Not counted as implemented                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [05:90-106](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/05-prep.md#L90)                        | P06/P07 new song and confirmed library deletion.                                          | No key behavior inferred beyond the user-decided shortcut policy.                                                               |
| [05:159-205](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/05-prep.md#L159)                      | Block edits, per-song history and label management.                                       | User-decided key bindings and section/drag rules are excluded.                                                                  |
| [05:503-617](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/05-prep.md#L503)                      | File/clipboard import, conversion preview, draft, save and duplicate handling.            | No unmounted Preferences page or remembered-last-import values.                                                                 |
| [10:854-856](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L854)    | Zoom numeric bounds/local storage; Korean-only runtime.                                   | Launch with Windows and the proposed General page are not mounted.                                                              |
| [10:868-876](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L868)    | Launch-output default and existing fixed transitions; active-output wake lock.            | No transition settings controls, preview-FPS consumer or hardware-acceleration preference consumer.                             |
| [10:890-893](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L890)    | Optional clear-undo visibility consumer.                                                  | Last-slide shortcut rule is user-decided; fade controls/defaults are not connected.                                             |
| [10:904-914](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L904)    | Runtime roots, automatic launch backup, retention and recovery copies.                    | No connected manual-backup action, backup warning notification, folder-picker/preferences page or folder-copy migration prompt. |
| [10:923-928](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L923)    | User-decided installed-translation model excluded.                                        | No proposed Bible defaults page; additional split/reference/name/max-line controls are not mounted.                             |
| [10:937-946](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L937)    | Import defaults consumed by the actual flow; conversion controls work.                    | Proposed preference bounds/page and remembered import fields are not implemented.                                               |
| [10:953-962](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L953)    | Managed-copy policy and new-media defaults; current audio runtime.                        | No copy-policy toggle, device selectors or return-to-saved-device fallback.                                                     |

| [10:1050-1081](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L1050) | Runtime style defaults, scaling and missing-font fallback.                                | No installed-font enumeration/style-editor controls or new proposed numeric ranges.                                             |

| [10:1210-1253](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L1210) | Current layout/media/sidebar/thumbnail fields stored through the app configuration owner. | No layout-reset action or complete proposed window/selection restoration matrix.                                                |
| [10:1264-1273](https://github.com/0913vision/llpresenter/blob/6440def62930896c2b2f4e2f4ca301c98a10ef68/docs/spec/10-shortcuts-and-settings.md#L1264) | Key-repeat guard, font fallback, native menu suppression and single instance.             | User key rules excluded; quit confirmation, Recycle Bin deletion and sibling relink are absent.                                 |

## Verification and limits

This is source-based QA. No app was launched, no live service data was touched,
and no runtime test is claimed for this document. File/line anchors, marker
coverage, unique row IDs, reference definitions and formatting are checked
before submission. Existing smoke results are not relabelled as a current-main
test. The lead reviews these sources before user-facing changes.
